PREFACE
(in original edition)

This work, which is now offered to the public, is believed to be the first attempt at a complete Dictionary of  the Tamil language in alphabetical order. It contins about 58,500 words being nearly four times as many as are found in the whole rJufuhâ.
When the work was commenced the Compilers contemplated preparing little more than a Vocabulary for the use of Schools.  This will account for the size of the Appendix in the vowels, and in f.
It is not supposed to be free from defects, but it is believed that the work will be highly valuable not only to those who read the Christian Scriptures in the Tamil language but to those who study the higher branches of Tamil literature.
AMERICAN MISSION PRESS,
JAFFNA, 1842



muṉṉuri (mūla nūl) : tamiḻākkam
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ colvaḷatti akaravariciyil muḻumi yākat tarum mutal muyaṟciyāka uruvākkappaṭṭa ivvakarāti tamiḻulakiṉ muṉṉar camarppikkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvakarātiyil 58,500 coṟkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; atāvatu caturakarāti muḻuvatilum aṭaṅkiya coṟkaḷiviṭa nāṉku maṭaṅku coṟkaḷ.
paḷḷi māṇava māṇaviyar payaṉpaṭuttum coṟṟokup pākat tāṉ ippaṇi mutaṟkaṇ toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. uyir mutaṟ coṟkaḷiṉ iṇippum kakarac coṟkaḷiṉ iṇippum caṟṟu nīḷamāka uḷḷataṟku atutāṉ kāṇaram.
innūlil piḻikaḷ cila irukkalām. eṉiṉum kiṟittava maṟi nūlkaḷit tamiḻil payilpavarkaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟi tamiḻ ilakkiyattiṉ palvēṟu nuṭpamāṉa tuṟikaḷil payilvōrukkum ivvakarāti perumpayaṉ taruvatāka irukkum eṉṟu nampap paṭukiṟatu.
1842.	viṭiyūḻiyar,
amerikkaṉ accakam, yāḻppāṇam,
 


īḻam tanta tamiḻk koṭi
īḻam tanta tamiḻkkoṭi peritu. īḻattup pūtantēvaṉār caṅka kālattavar. akkālattiṟkup piṉṉark kālam kālamākat tamiḻ vaḷartta muṉṉōṭiyarum pulamic cāṉṟōrum ēṟattāḻa irunūṟṟuvar uḷar. avaruḷ akarāti, kalik kaḷañciyam eṉpavaṟṟi muṉṉiṉṟu ceytu vaḻikāṭṭiya perumakkaḷ maṭṭum ivaṇ cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
“īḻam tanta iṉiya tamiḻk koṭi” eṉṉum peyarāl, īḻattut tamiḻc cāṉṟōr perumakkaḷ taṉi nūlākkam peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ne. cēṉāti rāya mutaliyār (1750 - 1840)
māṉippāy amerikkamicaṉ tokutta akarātit tokiyāḷaruḷ ivarum oruvar.
cimaṉ kācic ceṭṭi (1807 - 1861)
tamiḻp pulavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟi akarāti muṟiyil varicip paṭutti āṅkila nūl ceytavar ivar.
ivar ceyta innūlē pulavar varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpar. kūyaaṭi ñaṭaravayacuh eṉpatu ataṉpeyar. vinōtaraca mañcaripōl kaṭṭuk katiyallāta varalāṟu atu.

a. catācivap piḷḷi (1820 - 1896)
cimaṉ kācic ceṭṭi āṅkilattil ceyta tamiḻp pulavar varalāṟṟu nūli virivu ceytu ‘pāvalar carittira tīpakam’ eṉṉum peyaril tamiḻ moḻi peyarppāka veḷiyiṭṭavar ivar (1886)
cantira cēkara paṇṭitar (19ām nū.ā)
caturakarātiyi aṭuttuvanta akarāti yāḻppāṇa akarāti ākum. ataṉi māṉippāy akarāti eṉṟum colvar. ataṉi ākkutaṟkup peritum uḻittavar ippaṇṭitar.
pīṟṟar percival
īḻattil vāḻnta ivar, tamiḻp paḻamoḻikaḷit tokuttu āṅkila moḻipeyarppuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭār. āṅkila - tamiḻ akarāti oṉṟum veḷiyiṭṭār.
ku. katirivēṟ piḷḷi (1829 - 1904)
coṟkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, ilakkiya āṭci mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṭṭip pērakarāti ceytavar. maturit tamiḻc caṅkap paṇikku avvakarātiyāl perumi cērttavar.
nā. katirivēṟ piḷḷi (1844 - 1907)
ivarum pērakarāti oṉṟu ceytavar.
ā. muttut tampip piḷḷi (1852 - 1917)
ivar iyaṟṟiya apitāṉa kōcam eṉpatu tamiḻil tōṉṟiya kalik kaḷañciya nūlkaḷukku muṉṉōṭiyāṉatu (1902) āṅkilam - tamiḻ akarāti oṉṟum ivar ceytār.
cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācar (1875 - 1947)
tamiḻip piṟamoḻikaḷoṭum oppiṭṭu ‘ulakat talimoḻi tamiḻē’ eṉpati niṟuviyavar. coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali amittu veḷiyiṭṭavar. ‘tamiḻ amippuṟṟa varalāṟu’ varintavar.
na.ci. kantiyā piḷḷi (1893 - 1967)
centamiḻ akarāti, tamiḻp pulavar akarāti, tamiḻ ilakkiya akarāti, kālak kuṟippu akarāti, tirukkuṟaḷ collaṭivu akarāti eṉap pala akarātikaḷic ceyta ariya periya uḻippāḷar.

irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār

 


patippuri
akarāti eṉpatu, kaṟpārkkut tuṇiniṉṟu uriya poruḷ terintu koḷḷa vaḻivakuppatu; kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyariṉ niliyil uḷḷatu akarāti. āciriyariṭam nēril māṇavar kalviyiṉ payaṉip peṟuvatupōla akarātiyiṉ tuṇiyāl aṟivu viḷakkam peṟalām. akarāti eṉpati ‘moḻiñāyiṟu’ tēvanēyap pāvāṇar avarkaḷ  ‘akaramutali’ eṉṟu peyariṭṭu mutaṉmutalil tamiḻ ulakil puḻakkattukku viṭṭa perumik kuriyavar.
mutal tamiḻ akarāti - caturakarāti
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ittāli maṇṇiliruntu kiṟittava camayam parappat tamiḻmaṇṇukku vantavar peski pātiriyār eṉṉum vīramāmuṉivar. nikaṇṭukaḷilum, pā vaṭivilum amintirunta coṟporuḷ viḷakkaṅkaḷi mutaṉmutalāka uri naṭiyil māṟṟi ‘akarātiyāka’ 1732ām āṇṭu paṭittaḷittār. ip paṭippiṟkuc caturakarāti eṉap peyariṭṭār. ip paṭippu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappeṟṟa pōtilum muḻumiyāka 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 1824il tāṉ accil  veḷivantatu.  tamiḻ akarāti varalāṟṟil tiruppumuṉikku aṭikōliyavar vīramāmuṉivar.  
tamiḻīḻa aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyāviṉ aṉittu nūlkaḷiyum tēṭi eṭuttu  orē nērattil amiḻtam patippakam  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. ivar nūlkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kālak kuṟippu akarātiyip paṭikka nērntapōtu, 1842il cantira cēkarap paṇṭitar maṟṟum caravaṇamuttup piḷḷi avarkaḷāl  tokukkappaṭṭa māṉippāy akarāti eṉṉum yāḻppāṇa akarātiyip paṟṟiya kuṟippu kiṭittatu.  

tamiḻ akarātikaḷukku aṭittaḷam yāḻppāṇa akarāti
yāḻppāṇattic cērnta māṉippāy eṉṉum ūril acciṭappeṟṟatāl ūrppeyaric cuṭṭi ippeyar iṭap peṟṟatu pōlum. caturakarātiyiṉ mutal akarātiyākiya peyarakarātiyiṉ virivē yāḻppāṇa akarātiyākum.
19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ miyap pakutiyil 1842il ivvakarāti veḷivantatu. veḷivanta kālattil tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ niliyum, im moḻiyiṉpāl makkaḷiṉ pārviyum eppaṭi iruntaṉa eṉpati aṟiya utavuvatu yāḻppāṇa akarāti.. vaṭamoḻi vallāṇmi mēlōṅki irunta kālattil veḷivanta akarāti yātalāl mūlattil evvita māṟṟamum ceyyap paṭāmal marapu karuti uḷḷavāṟē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. ‘aṉupanta akarāti’ eṉṟa piṉṉiṇippu intap patippil akaravaricippaṭi avvav viṭattil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tāymoḻiyām tamiḻmoḻi uyarcciyil paṟṟuṭiya aṉparkaḷ aṉivarukkum payaṉpaṭumpaṭi ivvakarātiyi  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm.
aṇmik kālattil tamiḻp patippulakil paḻantamiḻ akarātikaḷ paṭaaccup patippāka paravalāka veḷi vantuḷḷa cūḻalil, iṉṟiya aṟiviyaliṉ tuṇi  koṇṭu accut tuṟiyiṉ vaḷarcci mēlōṅki irukkum niliyil kaṇiṉi vaḻi puttuyir peṟṟu mikac ciṟanta vaṭivamippuṭaṉ, innūliṉit tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku putiya varavāyk koṭut tuḷḷōm. tamiḻ akarātikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyi virumpuvārkkup paḻiya akarātikaḷ vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkāy amiyum. nam pāṭṭaṉ tanta aruntamiḻc celvattit tēṭi eṭuttut tamiḻarkkuk karuvūlamāyt tantuḷḷōm.
tamiḻ iḷantalimuṟikkum, āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, kallūri, palkalikkaḻaka nūlakaṅkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭum eṉum nōkkil 2006il veḷivanta ivvaruntamiḻ pērakarātiyi mīḷ patippāka ivvāṇṭu veḷiyiṭukiṟōm.
  tamiḻar tam nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷit tamiḻarkkuk koṇṭu cērkkum perum paṇiyi tolinōkkup pārvi yuṭaṉ em patippuc cuvaṭukaḷi patittu varukiṟōm. ulakeṅkum parantu vāḻum em tamiḻp pēriṉam akara mutali tuṟikku tiṟavu kōlāka uḷḷa ivvaruntamiḻ akarātiyi vāṅkip payaṉaṭiyum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm. ip paḻantamiḻp peṭṭakam putuppolivōṭu varuvataṟku tōḷōṭu tōḷ niṉṟu uḻitta aṉivarukkum em naṉṟi.

patippāḷar
